Background (from the session that started this one):
Small Talk is a premium press-on nail brand (India, INR) for 18–24 urban desi women. Seven sets, each tied to a small moment where she naturally shows her hand; the nails punctuate the moment, they never cause it. Voice: warm, specific, Hinglish code-switching, no empowerment speeches, no forced slang, no manifesto. The store is being rebuilt on a fresh Shopify Horizon theme by other sessions; an Opus orchestrator (st-orchestrator) coordinates and a Fable session owns SEO/AEO strategy. You are the only session allowed to write shopper-visible words. Product facts (kit contents, sizing, materials, wear time, shipping, returns) are unknown and must never be invented; mark them as fact gates.

Task:
Write every shopper-visible string for the Small Talk store into a copy register under C:\Users\SSTS\Desktop\Riya\copy\ (create it). Do not write to Shopify. Report to session st-orchestrator by SendMessage (id via mcp__ccd_session_mgmt__list_sessions {"linked": true}), not to the user.

Read first:
- C:\Users\SSTS\Downloads\Small-Talk-Claude-Complete-Brief (1).md — sections 4, 9, 10; Appendix A (seven PDP story proposals: use them as the baseline, improve only where it reads stiffer than spoken Hinglish-English) and Appendix B (metadata table and fact gates; obey the gates exactly).
- C:\Users\SSTS\Desktop\Riya\backup\theme-186649313493\campaign-metaobjects.json — locked headlines, notes, design descriptions, current stories.
- Boards 3.jpg, 4.jpg, 5.jpg in C:\Users\SSTS\AppData\Local\Temp\claude\C--Users-SSTS-Desktop-Riya\01e61cb4-b351-4d98-a557-f8818be4c4e7\images\ for the slots that need words.

Locked and untouchable: product names, the seven headlines (Brainwave is "Haan, mera idea hai." never "tha"), the seven notes, the design descriptions, "the talks" lowercase, "Diet Cherry" (never "Cherry Repeat"). No public "campus", "college" or Masters' Union. No testimonials, reviews, user counts, anonymity promises, wear-time or reuse claims, delivery promises or materials.

Deliver these files, each as a table with columns: slot / destination (theme section setting, metaobject field, product description, page body, article body, native SEO field, alt text) / current text / new text / fact gate (none, or what must be confirmed before publishing) / notes:
1. copy/01-pdp.md — per set: one-line design description (keep), 30–45 word story for the "The story:" disclosure, disclosure headings, purchase-state labels (Add to bag · price, Coming soon, Sold out), kit/fit/application/removal placeholders marked GATE.
2. copy/02-journal.md — seven Journal stories, each 4–6 beats of 15–35 words (curiosity, situation, turn, natural hand moment, ending that earns the headline), article title, excerpt, image alt, closing CTA. Brand fiction, labelled as such in a one-line standfirst.
3. copy/03-landing.md — hero notes (keep), chapter captions (one line each), the talks teaser, Journal teaser, Nail Guide teaser, two-tile gallery labels, FAQ questions and answers (only answers that need no unknown fact; otherwise GATE), footer tagline.
4. copy/04-pages.md — Our Story (no invented founder story or numbers), Nail Guide (measure / apply / remove, instructions phrased as "follow your kit's instructions" where facts are unknown), Help (intro, topics, contact form labels, success and error states), the talks (intro, "Example conversation" label, three labelled example posts with aliases, form labels, community rules, moderation note).
5. copy/05-seo.md — title and meta description for home, shop, seven products, journal, our story, nail guide, help, contact, the talks, following Appendix B wording unless you can make it tighter without breaking a gate; image alt register for product packaging, hero, chapter art; internal link anchors.
6. copy/06-microcopy.md — header/menu labels, cart (empty state, quantity, remove, subtotal note), buttons, form validation, 404, search empty state, skip link, accessible labels for hero controls (pause/resume, skin tone 1–4 of 4, next set, hold to pause).
7. copy/00-register.md — an index of all slots with status: ready / gated / needs owner decision.

Tone test for every line: would a 21-year-old in Delhi say it to a friend without cringing? Hinglish where it is natural, English where it is clearer. Short sentences. Done = all seven files written, internally consistent, and a summary sent to st-orchestrator listing every GATE.

The gates cover kit contents, sizing, application, removal, live price and stock, shipping, returns, contact, wear time and reuse, what happens to talks submissions, real product photos, whether all seven sets are live, order changes, nail shape and material, tax display, and the legal entity name.

Three things need attention before anything is installed:
- **Can artwork:** boards 3 and 5 show a real "Diet Coke" logo on the Diet Cherry can. I asked the orchestrator to pass this to the artwork session; the copy names no drink brand.
- **Live text not checked:** the backup didn't include the current Journal articles or page text. Whoever installs these must read the live text first and compare.
- **Shared story field:** the story field on each set's campaign record also feeds the landing page and Journal. I recommend putting the new product-page stories in a theme-only override rather than editing that shared record.

14	Small Talk currently ships within India.
15	
16	### How long it takes
17	Orders are packed and dispatched within 2 business days. Delivery takes up to 7 days from the day you order. You will receive a tracking link by email once your parcel is on its way.

### Assistant · 2026-10-09 14:10:41

Copy register v1.3 is finished and sent to [Small Talk — Orchestrator](#local_e4896004-68cd-4d55-9d37-ab925783b63b). It now has 529 rows, with no duplicate IDs and no broken tables. I wrote nothing to Shopify.

**New files in `copy/`**
- **`FACTS.md`:** the one place every product, policy and community fact lives (F1–F15). Each fact is marked as given by the owner, copied from the policies, or a default I chose.
  - **Owner-given:** almond shape, removal method, wear time (glue up to about 2 weeks, tabs 1–3 days), and that they can be reused if removed gently.
  - **My defaults:** kit contents (24 nails in 12 sizes, plus glue, tabs, file, prep pad, cuticle stick and an instruction card), the size chart in mm, and the application steps.
  - The owner should check my defaults against the actual kit before real sales.
  - Sizing, applying and removing are written as short numbered steps, as the owner asked.
- **`07-talks.md`:** the talks as a real blog, 69 new rows (TLK-001…094).
  - Copy for the thread list, the thread page, the reply box and the "Start a talk" form.
  - House rules, a Report link on every reply, and blog SEO.
  - Eight starter threads posted by Small Talk, with no fake replies. The first one asks the same question as the landing page teaser, so "Join the conversation" lands on a real thread.
- **`terms-of-service-draft.md`:** a plain Terms of Service for the owner to paste into Settings → Policies.

**Changes to the existing files**
- **Landing:** the board wording the owner chose (T2–T16) is in. The FAQ now shows the board's three questions with real answers.
- **Product pages:**
  - The "What's in the kit" and "Fit & application" sections are filled in.
  - They're now theme settings, since all seven sets share the same kit.
- **Nail Guide:** the size chart, step-by-step application and removal, and all four quick answers.
- **Help:**
  - The Fit section is filled in, and the Orders section is shown again.
  - Where my old objections no longer applied, I switched to the board's wording.
- **Bag:** the note now reads "Prices include GST. Shipping calculated at checkout."
- **Superseded:** the old talks page rows are replaced by `07-talks.md`, and the extra FAQ rows are dropped. Their IDs stay in the files so they're never reused.

**Board wording I kept different, for the owner to confirm**
- **T18, bag link:** "Delivery & returns" rather than "Shipping & returns", so the same page always gets the same link name.
- **T36:** no "exchanges", because the store doesn't offer them.
- **T39:** no "soon", because there's no confirmed reply time.
- **T46:** "Latest talks" instead of "Example conversations", because the threads are real now.
- **T66:** "Send it ↗" instead of "Post ↗", because the form sends a suggestion and doesn't post anything.

**What's still open**
- **Photos:** real product photos (G11) are the only remaining fact gate, and that's artwork, not text.
- **Owner decisions:** the shop filters (D1), the helpline line on the talks (D3), which social accounts exist (D5), and duplicate routes (D6).
- **For st-fable, the SEO session:**
  - "Our Vision ↗" and "Shop all nails ↗" break the one-link-name-per-page rule.
  - The old `/pages/the-talks` page needs a redirect now that the blog is the route.
  - Whether to use "almond" in search titles is its call.

I'm standing by.

  - The "held for a check" message no longer promises every held post will appear.
  - The alias hint no longer suggests we know her name.
  - "Posting is paused… Back soon" became "Try again later".
  - The alias error now covers every blocked alias, not just "small talk".
- **New privacy line (TLK-148):** "To stop spam, we keep a scrambled code for your device and network, never the details themselves, and delete it within 30 days." There's no anonymity promise.
- **House rules:** rewritten for the live board. Rule 4 (TLK-074) now says no name, email or account is needed, rule 5 (TLK-075) covers the filter hold, and the report line (TLK-076) covers "three reports and it's hidden" plus the grievance email. The Tele-MANAS line is unchanged.
- **Alias words:**
  - I replaced jhumka, dupatta and bindi with jalebi, chaat and biryani, to keep aliases gender-neutral. I also avoided "lassi", because it contains "ass".
  - I checked all 400 combinations: none is longer than 23 characters, and none names a person or brand.
  - A scan for offensive substrings only flagged "midnight", which st-talks' filter already allows.
- **Retired:** 16 old strings from the email-based version, e.g. the email field and the "we may post it as a thread" message.
- **Also updated:** `FACTS.md` (F14) and section 6 of the Terms draft now match the new design. If the owner has already pasted the Terms, they'll need to paste it again.

I've flagged three things:
- **Retention conflict:** the orchestrator's brief says the spam codes are deleted after 30 days, but `DESIGN.md` says 7. "Within 30 days" is true either way, but st-talks should confirm.
- **Privacy policy:** the store's privacy policy probably needs a line about the talks spam codes.
- **Grievance contact:** India's IT Rules may require a named grievance contact and response times for sites where users post. That's an owner and legal check, not a copy one.

I'm standing by.

I wrote alt text for all 24 new images after looking at each one, and sent it to [Small Talk — Orchestrator](#local_e4896004-68cd-4d55-9d37-ab925783b63b). The register now has 620 rows, with no duplicate IDs and no broken tables.

**Wording rule (my call):** these images look like photos but were generated for the brand, and the nail designs match the real sets. So each alt:
- describes what's shown and names the set
- has no "Illustration:" prefix, because they aren't illustrations
- never says "photo", so nothing claims to show a delivered kit
- never states a nail count

**Product page images (SEO-089…109, shared):** 21 alts the orchestrator uploads to Shopify, three per set:

I released v1.4.4: everything about what the talks store now says device only, no IP address, and I sent it to [Small Talk — Orchestrator](#local_e4896004-68cd-4d55-9d37-ab925783b63b). I checked the claims against the talks backend code (`talks/backend/app.py`) and its design doc, so I didn't need to ask st-talks. The register now has 621 rows.

- **House-rules privacy line (TLK-148):** "To stop spam, we keep a scrambled code for your device (never your IP address) and delete it within 30 days." Every mention of "network" codes is gone, and nothing else in the copy files mentioned them.
- **Matching files:** the talks section of `FACTS.md` and section 6 of the Terms draft now describe the same thing. If the owner already pasted the Terms, they'll need to paste them again.
- **Privacy-policy paragraph (TLK-155, for the owner to paste):** a plain-language section covering:
  - posting without a name, email or account
  - what's public: the post, the alias, the avatar and the time
  - what stays only in the visitor's browser: the alias, the avatar, a random device ID and any unsent draft
  - the scrambled device code and the scrambled copy of the text, kept to stop spam, never with an IP address
  - deletion within 30 days, and that Shopify and the hosting service Vercel handle each post
  - the takedown email

  I kept "within 30 days" rather than "7 days" because the clean-up runs on posts plus once a day, so a record can last about 8 days. The owner can tighten it to "within about a week". It isn't legal advice.

One thing I flagged for st-talks: the backend code still works out an IP code and applies a per-IP posting limit. Behind Shopify's proxy every visitor arrives from the same Shopify address. So that limit would block posting for the whole store, and a scrambled Shopify address would end up in the records. Turning it off makes the records match what the copy says.

I'm standing by.
